<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.businessinsider.com/nvidia-in-talks-to-buy-hugging-face-13-billion-dollars-2026-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia agrees to acquire Hugging Face for $13B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达同意以130亿美元收购 Hugging Face</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 897</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49458161' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA plans to buy Hugging Face for $13 billion, positioning its software ecosystem to ride alongside its hardware stack. The acquisition would deepen integration of models, datasets, and tools across the NVIDIA platform, potentially accelerating AI deployment and the competitive dynamics of AI infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达以130亿美元收购 Hugging Face，意在把开源模型生态融入自家硬件与软件工具链。交易可能加速 AI 部署并改变 AI 基础设施的竞争格局，同时影响开源社区的格局。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别做肉身代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 710</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49151933' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Avoid being used as a proxy for others’ actions or identity in tech systems; protect your own data and autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不要让自己被作为他人行为或身份的代理，在技术系统中保持对自身数据和行为的控制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49303202' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49303202' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcworld.com/article/3212428/firefox-is-now-the-last-major-browser-that-still-supports-ublock-origin.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox is now the last major browser that still supports uBlock Origin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 成为仍支持 uBlock Origin 的最后一款主流浏览器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 672</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49303202' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox remains the last major browser to support uBlock Origin, as others shift away from traditional ad-block extensions due to evolving extension policies. This preserves a familiar privacy tool for Firefox users but underscores growing fragmentation in how browsers handle ad blocking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 仍然是主流浏览器中最后一个支持 uBlock Origin 的选择。随着其他浏览器因扩展策略和 MV3 变更而削弱对这类扩展的支持，Firefox 的隐私保护能力仍具吸引力，但也暴露出浏览器扩展生态的分化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/3212428-0-62401400-1786636364-Firefox-foto-mozilla.png?w=1024' data-full='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/3212428-0-62401400-1786636364-Firefox-foto-mozilla.png?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49379550' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49379550' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.curiousquail.com/im-upset-again-about-a-co-creator-of-rss-being-prosecuted-for-something-meta-is-doing-with-little-consequence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aaron Swartz was prosecuted for scraping, while Meta does it without consequence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Aaron Swartz 因抓取被起诉，而 Meta 却未受惩罚</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 385</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49379550' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aaron Swartz faced a high-profile prosecution for data scraping, while large platforms like Meta navigate similar tactics with far fewer legal repercussions. The contrast underscores how enforcement risk impacts researchers, developers, and data-access culture in the web era. This discrepancy influences open access, data mining, and how future scrapers are treated legally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这凸显了对数据抓取的执法不对称——个人开发者可能遭到起诉，而大型科技公司常获较少或无惩罚。事件影响数据获取与开放获取的格局，也对未来的合规风险和研究活动产生深远意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/curiousquail/img_2026_web.jpg' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/curiousquail/img_2026_web.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49419237' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lectronz.com/u/lectronz/articles/how-europe-is-killing-makers-and-micro-entrepreneurs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Europe is killing makers and micro-entrepreneurs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲如何扼杀制造者和微型创业者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49419237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49419237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1011</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49419237' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe is tightening rules that raise compliance costs for small-scale makers and micro-entrepreneurs. The higher friction could slow hardware startups and push more innovation to friendlier regions, reshaping local maker ecosystems. This trend matters for hardware, IoT, and regional tech communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲正在收紧法规，提升小规模制造者和微型创业者的合规成本。更高的摩擦可能放缓硬件创业，并将更多创新挪向更友好的地区，重塑本地创客生态。此趋势关系到硬件、物联网以及区域科技社群。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenLogi 提供面向開發者的物流堆疊，通過 API、庫存路由和多個倉配連接來簡化電商履約流程。它讓商家在不自建後端物流的情況下，也能協調分散倉儲和出貨。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8 27B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8-27B 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49299605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49299605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 782</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49299605' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 marks another step toward accessible, efficient LLMs. The 27B parameter model is optimized for FP8 inference, enabling faster runs on commodity GPUs and lower memory footprints, helping hobbyists and small teams experiment with large models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 提供更高效的推理，27亿参数在普通显卡上也能更快地跑，显存需求更低，便于个人和小团队尝试大模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8.png' data-full='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49345263' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49345263' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seths.blog/2026/08/the-amazon-tax/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Amazon tax</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亞馬遜稅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 686</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49345263' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Selling on Amazon often requires giving up margin, control, and customer relationships to platform fees, advertising costs, and algorithmic visibility. That cumulative cost — the &#x27;Amazon tax&#x27; — forces sellers to optimize for the marketplace&#x27;s incentives rather than their own brand or customers, with long-term competitive and dependency risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在亞馬遜上銷售意味著要承擔平臺費用、廣告支出以及算法能見度帶來的成本，從而侵蝕利潤並削弱與顧客的直接關係。這種「亞馬遜稅」會讓賣家為了迎合平臺而非建立自有品牌或體驗，增加長期依賴與競爭風險。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seths.blog/wp-content/uploads/2018/06/seth_godin_ogimages_v02_18061312.jpg' data-full='https://seths.blog/wp-content/uploads/2018/06/seth_godin_ogimages_v02_18061312.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49161518' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49161518' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seangoedecke.com/llms-reward-expertise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs reward expertise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大模型奖励专业知识</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 563</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49161518' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent work highlights that LLMs tend to reward content produced by or aligned with domain expertise. This bias emphasizes high-quality data and expert sources in training and evaluation, shaping model behavior and prompts. The takeaway: invest in expert-curated data and robust evaluation to avoid gatekeeping and misalignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明，大型语言模型倾向于奖励来自专业领域的高质量内容，这种偏好来自数据质量和专家来源在训练及评估中的重要性，进而影响模型行为与提示设计。核心意义在于应重视专家数据的整合与严格评估，避免因过度依赖权威而带来评判偏差或门槛化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49433292' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49433292' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple introduces M6 and M5 Ultra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果推出 M6 与 M5 Ultra 引领性能与 AI 算力大跃升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49433292' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple claims M6 and M5 Ultra deliver a substantial jump in CPU/GPU performance and on‑device AI compute, enabling heavier workloads like real‑time inference and larger models at the edge. The chips are expected to bring higher memory bandwidth and improved neural accelerators, reinforcing Apple’s pro workflow ambitions across its lineup. For developers and creators, this sets a new baseline for on‑device AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果称 M6 与 M5 Ultra 将在 CPU/GPU 性能与边缘 AI 算力方面实现显著跃升，使实时推理和更大模型在本地可运行。新芯片预计带来更高的内存带宽与改进的神经处理单元，强化其在 Pro 级工作流中的布局。对于开发者与创作者来说，标志着本地 AI 能力的新基线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/tile/Apple-M6-and-M5-Ultra-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/tile/Apple-M6-and-M5-Ultra-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>令人毛骨悚然的爬虫</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49388154' data-tags='Design,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49388154' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kagi.com/changelog#11296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kagi added a setting for removing paywalled links from search results</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kagi 新增可去除付费墙链接的搜索设置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 390</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49388154' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kagi added a setting to filter paywalled links from search results, giving users more control over what shows up. The feature helps researchers and readers who need free access and could pressure publishers to rethink paywalls. It highlights a shift toward user-tuned search experiences and more transparent results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kagi 增加了从搜索结果中筛除付费墙链接的设置，让用户对显示内容有更大控制。该功能方便需要免费访问的研究者与读者，同时也可能促使出版商重新考虑付费墙策略。此举体现了向用户定制化搜索体验和更透明结果的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kagi.com/asset/bd30142/img/press/social/discord.svg?v=6fc1d4eb38fe39498eb10315e50c3f36b4fde712' data-full='https://kagi.com/asset/bd30142/img/press/social/discord.svg?v=6fc1d4eb38fe39498eb10315e50c3f36b4fde712' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49503601' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49503601' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/i-just-chose-words-carefully/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“I just chose words carefully”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我只是认真地挑选了措辞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49503601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49503601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49503601' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Careful word choice shapes how tech ideas are understood and trusted, influencing user perception and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在科技语境中，措辞的慎密选择决定了人们对技术理念的理解和信任度，影响公众感知与问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/i-just-chose-words-carefully/ogimage.jpg' data-full='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/i-just-chose-words-carefully/ogimage.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49272832' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49272832' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/sqlite-wal-reset-bug' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracking down the 16-year-old WAL-reset SQLite bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪这起持续16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49272832' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers zero in on a long-standing SQLite WAL reset bug that has persisted for 16 years. The issue can cause data integrity problems when the write-ahead logging file resets under certain conditions, impacting apps using WAL mode. The takeaway is to test WAL edge cases and ensure robust backup/restore paths, highlighting SQLite quirks in real-world apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员锁定一宗持续了16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞。该问题在特定条件下会导致写入前日志文件重置时的数据完整性问题，影响使用 WAL 模式的应用程序。建议开发者测试 WAL 的边界情况，并确保备份/恢复路径健全，凸显实际应用中 SQLite 的潜在坑点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=45389fc1-da6e-4e0d-82f6-140651c6ba57' data-full='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=45389fc1-da6e-4e0d-82f6-140651c6ba57' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49241679' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49241679' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.meta.ai/blog/introducing-muse-glimmer-open-agentic-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Glimmer: 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Glimmer：面向持续本地代理工作流优化的30B参数模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49241679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 634</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49241679' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse Glimmer is a 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows, enabling persistent autonomous agents with privacy-friendly, low-latency inference. It embodies a shift toward on-device or edge AI that reduces reliance on cloud services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse Glimmer 是一个为持续本地代理工作流优化的 30B 参数模型，支持持续自我驱动的代理并具备隐私友好、低延迟的推理能力。这反映了在本地或边缘部署 AI、减少对云端依赖的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=2272911630133843&amp;version=1786289182' data-full='https://lookaside.fbsbx.com/elementpath/media/?media_id=2272911630133843&amp;version=1786289182' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49195231' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49195231' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mayerowitz.io/blog/mario-meets-pareto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mario Meets Pareto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马里奥遇见帕累托原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49195231' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful piece that uses Mario to illustrate Pareto efficiency in optimization, offering intuition for how a small subset of actions can drive most outcomes in complex environments. The concept translates to AI planning and resource allocation, guiding developers toward high-impact, scalable decisions. It’s a reminder that clever prioritization often beats brute-force attempts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过以马里奥为例来阐释帕累托效率在优化中的作用，帮助读者理解在复杂环境中少数行动如何推动大部分结果。这一思路可迁移到 AI 规划与资源分配，指引开发者关注高影响力、可扩展的决策。提醒人们，精明的优先排序往往胜过盲目扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mayerowitz.io/SEO/mario-meets-pareto.png' data-full='https://www.mayerowitz.io/SEO/mario-meets-pareto.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49294997' data-tags='AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49294997' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://z.ai/blog/glm-5.3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.3: Frontier coding with emergent cyber capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.3：具备涌现性网络能力的前沿编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49294997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 557</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49294997' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.3 expands the frontier of AI-assisted coding by showcasing emergent cyber capabilities that go beyond standard copilots. It hints at advanced code generation, reasoning, and integration skills that could reshape developer workflows and security tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.3 展示了超越常规编码助手的涌现性网络能力，推动代码生成、推理与系统集成等方面的进步。此举可能重新定义开发者工作流以及相关的安全工具链。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.3-Flash</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49449507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49449507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49449507' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new release in the GLM-5.3 family focusing on cost-efficient inference, likely leveraging quantization and optimization tricks to run capable models on cheaper hardware. This broadens practical deployment of large language models on commodity devices and smaller clusters. The trend continues: vendors pushing cheaper, accessible LLMs without sacrificing too much accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.3-Flash 发布，强调在低成本硬件上的高效推理，可能通过量化与优化实现更便宜设备上的可用模型。此举扩展了大模型在普通硬件/小型集群上的落地场景，体现了厂商在降低部署成本上的持续努力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49448321' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ducklabs.com/news/2026/08/26/ducklabs-to-join-aws' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS Acquires DuckLabs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS 收购 DuckLabs</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1078</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49448321' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Web Services has acquired DuckLabs to bolster its data tooling and AI capabilities. The acquisition signals AWS&#x27;s push to integrate DuckLabs&#x27; technology into its cloud platform, potentially expanding its offerings for data-driven workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊网络服务宣布收购 DuckLabs，以增强其数据工具和人工智能能力。这笔交易可能将把 DuckLabs 的技术融入 AWS 的云平台，拓展数据驱动工作负载的能力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49437283' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/issues/1442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter and XCancel receive cease and desist notices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 收到停止令通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49437283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1076</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49437283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 910</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49437283' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Independent Twitter frontends like Nitter and XCancel have received cease-and-desist notices. The action underscores ongoing legal pressure on third-party clients that access a platform&#x27;s content and raises questions about privacy-focused, lightweight access versus platform control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立的 Twitter 前端 Nitter 与 XCancel 收到停止令，凸显第三方前端在访问平台内容时面临的法律压力。此举引发对隐私导向、轻量级访问与平台控制之间平衡的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7d6bb5c1b3e61754726cd6b301199636ea37ef501d250bf15da38b61c8934e6e/zedeus/nitter/issues/1442' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7d6bb5c1b3e61754726cd6b301199636ea37ef501d250bf15da38b61c8934e6e/zedeus/nitter/issues/1442' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49386895' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49386895' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/08/21/us/politics/samuel-tunick-deleted-phone-felony.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Felony charges for citizen deleting phone data at US Border</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在美边境删除手机数据者被控重罪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49386895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1071</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49386895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49386895' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US citizen is charged with a felony after deleting data from a phone at the border. Prosecutors say the act violated federal law and could carry serious penalties, underscoring tensions between border enforcement and digital privacy. The case may shape how data handling at crossings is treated in court and could affect travelers&#x27; rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名美国公民在边境处删除手机数据后被控以重罪。检方称此举违反联邦法并可能面临严重刑罚，凸显边境执法与数字隐私之间的紧张关系。此案可能影响法院对跨境数据处理的判决及旅客权利。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49254880' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49254880' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lemonde.fr/en/france/article/2026/08/06/france-to-ban-unsolicited-telemarketing-calls-from-august-11_6756208_7.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France to ban unsolicited telemarketing calls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国将全面禁止未经同意的电话推销</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1035</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49254880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49254880' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France plans to ban unsolicited telemarketing calls starting August 11, tightening consumer protection. The policy could reshape how marketers reach customers, boosting opt-in channels and call-blocking solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国将于11日起全面禁止未经请求的电话推销，强化对消费者隐私的保护。该举措可能促使企业转向获同意的联系渠道，并影响营销行业的电话营销策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lemonde.fr/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://www.lemonde.fr/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49337392' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://responsiblestatecraft.org/israel-influence-chatgpt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israel creates fake think tank in likely attempt to dupe AI chatbots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列创建假智库，可能意在欺骗 AI 聊天机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49337392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1028</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49337392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 777</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49337392' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A state-backed fake think tank was allegedly set up to feed misleading information to AI chatbots. The episode underscores how misinformation can seep into AI systems and policy discussions, highlighting the need for source verification in AI training data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，一家由国家背景支持的假智库被设立用于向 AI 聊天机器人提供误导信息。此事件揭示了错误信息如何进入 AI 系统和政策讨论，凸显对训练数据源进行核验的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://responsiblestatecraft.org/media-library/screenshot-of-the-hanover-institute-s-website-taken-on-aug-16-2026.png?id=67630219&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C0%2C0%2C65' data-full='https://responsiblestatecraft.org/media-library/screenshot-of-the-hanover-institute-s-website-taken-on-aug-16-2026.png?id=67630219&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C0%2C0%2C65' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49274600' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases V4 Pro 0813, a major update that enhances on-device AI performance and search speed. The release focuses on faster indexing, improved privacy controls, and enterprise-friendly features for large-scale data workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 发布 V4 Pro 0813，显著提升本地 AI 性能与检索速度，强调更快的索引与更强的隐私控制，并为大规模数据工作流提供企业级特性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49209539' data-tags='Career,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49209539' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.noemamag.com/why-is-everyone-in-tech-so-sad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happens if an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当整类工人对职业失去信心时会怎样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1023</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209539' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers, broad churn and talent shifts could disrupt project roadmaps and innovation cycles. The piece argues burnout, misaligned incentives, and perceived stagnation in tech drive people to seek meaning elsewhere, urging leaders to rethink career paths and well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果整类工人对职业前景失去信心，可能引发大量离职与技能转型，影响项目进度与创新节奏。文章指出倦怠、激励错位和对职业成长的感知停滞促使人们寻求更有意义的工作，呼吁领导层重新设计职业发展路径与员工福祉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noemamag.imgix.net/2026/08/HR_Knitting_Noema_LeonieBos-copy.jpg?fit=crop&amp;fm=pjpg&amp;h=628&amp;ixlib=php-3.3.1&amp;rect=0%2C32%2C945%2C495&amp;w=1200&amp;wpsize=noema-social-facebook&amp;s=ff77fe7391cba53b40080273232ce909' data-full='https://noemamag.imgix.net/2026/08/HR_Knitting_Noema_LeonieBos-copy.jpg?fit=crop&amp;fm=pjpg&amp;h=628&amp;ixlib=php-3.3.1&amp;rect=0%2C32%2C945%2C495&amp;w=1200&amp;wpsize=noema-social-facebook&amp;s=ff77fe7391cba53b40080273232ce909' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49371857' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49371857' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dontpastetheai.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t paste the AI, please</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请勿直接粘贴 AI 内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49371857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1020</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49371857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 567</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49371857' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open campaign promoting better AI data handling and privacy, urging users not to paste prompts or model outputs into public chat interfaces. It highlights the growing need for policy and tooling to protect prompts, training data, and personal information. It also advocates for private or local prompt workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个倡导更好 AI 数据处理与隐私的公开运动，敦促用户不要把提示或模型输出粘贴到公开的对话界面。强调保护提示、训练数据与个人信息的必要性，并倡导私有或本地化的提示工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dontpastetheai.com/assets/og-image.png' data-full='https://dontpastetheai.com/assets/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49458418' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49458418' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SenteLabsAI/OpenExecutive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CEO fired developers to make room for AI. Developers create open source AI CEO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CEO 为了给 AI 腾出空间而解雇开发者，开发者推出开源 AI CEO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1008</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 698</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49458418' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CEO allegedly fired developers to make room for AI, prompting debates about AI governance and corporate strategy; in response, a team of developers created an open-source AI CEO project to explore decentralized AI leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称某CEO为让AI腾出空间而解雇开发者，引发关于 AI 治理与企业策略的讨论。开发者们进而推出了开源 AI CEO 项目（OpenExecutive），以探索去中心化的 AI 领导力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/915091b36381841d8490d5229248141b0a63a421110153eeeaf232fece964cc4/SenteLabsAI/OpenExecutive' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/915091b36381841d8490d5229248141b0a63a421110153eeeaf232fece964cc4/SenteLabsAI/OpenExecutive' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49479837' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49479837' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ckardaris.com/blog/2026/08/28/keyboard-driven-guis.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GUIs should be fully keyboard-driven</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GUI 应实现全键盘可用性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49479837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 997</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49479837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 490</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49479837' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates argue that GUIs should be navigable entirely via keyboard to boost accessibility and speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张将 GUI 完全通过键盘操作来实现可访问性与效率提升。文中提出若干设计模式与工具，帮助减少对鼠标的依赖，使应用对无障碍用户和高效工作流用户更友好。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49420873' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49420873' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/lemire/status/2091894299289874926' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xiaomi: New CPU matches Apple cores single threaded, much faster multithreaded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小米新 CPU：单核媲美苹果核心，多核性能显著提升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49420873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 993</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49420873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 723</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49420873' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new Xiaomi CPU delivers single-thread performance that rivals Apple cores, while delivering notably higher throughput on multi-threaded workloads. The design emphasis appears to optimize threading efficiency and instruction-level parallelism, challenging assumptions about relative performance across mobile SoCs. The result could shift expectations for mid-range and flagship Android devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新一代小米处理器在单线程性能上接近苹果核心，而在多线程工作负载上则显著更快。此设计似乎强调对线程效率和指令级并行性的优化，挑战了移动SoC之间性能的普遍认知。对于中高端安卓设备的期望可能因此发生变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HQfmx0UWoAAvbt6.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HQfmx0UWoAAvbt6.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49372583' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49372583' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.laserphile.com/2026/08/aliexpress-webpage-keeping-multipoint.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AliExpress runs silent WebAudio fingerprinting that breaks Bluetooth multipoint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AliExpress 静默进行 WebAudio 指纹识别，影响蓝牙多点连接</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49372583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 984</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49372583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49372583' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AliExpress pages reportedly perform silent WebAudio fingerprinting, interfering with Bluetooth multipoint connections. The finding highlights privacy risks and the stealthy nature of some tracking techniques on consumer sites.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 AliExpress 页面在后台进行 WebAudio 指纹识别，干扰蓝牙多点连接。这一发现凸显隐私风险及部分网站使用隐匿跟踪技术的现状。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' data-full='https://resources.blogblog.com/img/icon18_edit_allbkg.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49360015' data-tags='Politics,Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49360015' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sprocketfox.io/xssfox/2026/08/19/sondehub-and-war/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A joke domain purchase turned in geopolitical warfare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个玩笑域名购买引发的地缘政治冲突</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49360015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 980</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49360015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49360015' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A whimsical domain-name purchase spirals into geopolitical warfare, illustrating how digital assets can become leverage in information and power struggles. The tale underscores the political fragility and strategic significance of internet infrastructure and branding in a global context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个玩笑性质的域名购买竟演变成地缘政治对抗，说明数字资产可以成为信息与权力博弈中的筹码。此事凸显网络基础设施与品牌在全球格局中的政治敏感性与战略意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sprocketfox.io/xssfox/images/war.png' data-full='https://sprocketfox.io/xssfox/images/war.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49271994' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49271994' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.florianherrengt.com/ai-removing-middle-class-software-engineering.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is removing the middle class of software engineering?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在吞并软件工程的中产阶层</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 976</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49271994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 904</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49271994' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A viewpoint argues that AI is eroding the middle tier of software engineering by automating routine tasks and raising productivity. It questions career ladders and suggests that engineers must adapt by focusing on higher-level design, system architecture, and AI-augmented workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 正通过自动化日常任务和提高生产率，削弱软件工程的中层岗位。文章讨论职业晋升路径的变化，建议工程师在高层设计、系统架构以及与 AI 协作的工作流中寻找新机会。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49289112' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49289112' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-gemini-3-7-flash/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.7 Flash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.7 快闪版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 954</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 484</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49289112' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.7 Flash extends the Gemini line with a lighter, faster variant optimized for low latency and smaller memory usage. It underscores Google’s ongoing push to edge-ready AI and developer-friendly tooling for large models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.7 快闪版在保持核心能力的同时，提供更轻量、低延迟的推理性能，降低内存占用，便于边缘部署。此举显示谷歌在大模型的边缘化与开发者工具方面的持续推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-7-flash.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-7-flash.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49364559' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter announces a strategic move to join Stripe, expanding its payments infrastructure for developers. The collaboration signals Stripe’s continued push into developer platforms and fintech-enabled services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter 宣布将加入 Stripe，扩展其面向开发者的支付基础设施。此举反映 Stripe 在开发者生态和金融科技服务领域的持续扩张。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49362689' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows off surprising capabilities of plain HTML in building rich interfaces with minimal JavaScript.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了纯 HTML 在无需大量 JavaScript 的情况下实现丰富界面的能力。强调语义结构、可访问性和渐进增强是打造可靠、可扩展网页应用的强大工具。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discovery Loop captures an ongoing cycle of discovery and iteration at the heart of modern product and data workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发现循环聚焦于现代产品与数据工作流程中的持续探索与迭代这一核心循环。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49201970' data-tags='Hardware,AI,Startups,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49201970' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/systems/2026/08/06/amd-acquires-ai-chip-startup-taalas-to-boost-inference-performance-by-etching-models-into-silicon/5284344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD acquires Taalas to boost inference performance by etching models in silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD收购Taalas以通过在硅上刻画模型提升推理性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 687</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49201970' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD expands its AI hardware play by acquiring Taalas, a startup focused on embedding AI models directly into silicon. The deal aims to boost inference throughput and energy efficiency, signaling a trend toward hardware-software co-design for large-scale AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD通过收购Taalas，意在将AI模型直接嵌入芯片中以提升推理吞吐量与能效，显示出硬件与软件深度协同设计的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5284365.jpg?imageId=5284365&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5284365.jpg?imageId=5284365&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49250836' data-tags='Data,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49250836' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thewalrus.ca/google-search-is-dying/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>As AI eats the web, the internet’s collective memory is disappearing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随着 AI 吃掉网络，互联网的集体记忆正在消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 910</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 937</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49250836' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI consumes more of the web, the internet’s collective memory is thinning. The piece argues that pervasive AI crawling and caching threaten provenance and long-term memory, complicating future search and verification. It calls for durable archiving and mindful data sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 越学越多地抓取网络，互联网的集体记忆正在变薄。文章指出 AI 抓取与缓存的长期可追溯性下降，影响可验证的知识来源。呼吁加强长期存档和负责任的数据获取。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49296740' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49296740' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mun-logadan.github.io/why-does-opus-5-feel-worse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does Opus 5 feel worse to work with?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Opus 5 的使用感更差？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 902</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 808</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49296740' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis argues that Opus 5 feels worse to work with due to UX and workflow frictions common in enterprise software. It points to inconsistent interfaces, onboarding friction, and brittle customization that hamper daily productivity, despite feature-rich capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Opus 5 的工作体验为何较差进行了分析，指向用户体验不一致、工作流阻塞和隐藏的复杂性等问题，影响日常生产力。该案例也折射出企业级软件在可用性方面的不足，即便功能强大也难以落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mun-logadan.github.io/icons/search.svg' data-full='https://mun-logadan.github.io/icons/search.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49222189' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.senko.net/code-was-never-the-hard-part-is-an-insult-to-all-programmers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Code was never the hard part” is an insult to all programmers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 对所有程序员都是侮辱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 557</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49222189' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; is often cited as a simplification of software work, but a blog argues it insults programmers by overlooking design, coordination, and maintenance. The piece reframes what makes projects hard and where real value lies for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 常被用来简化软件工作，但一篇博文认为这句话是在贬损程序员，因为它忽略了设计、协作与维护等真正的挑战。文章重新聚焦于影响项目难度与价值的关键因素。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49468083' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49468083' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/dns-cache-memory-optimization-1111/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Saving 100 terabytes of memory by optimizing 1.1.1.1&#x27;s DNS cache</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过优化 1.1.1.1 的 DNS 缓存节省 100 TB 内存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49468083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 899</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49468083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49468083' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare details how reworking DNS cache structures and eviction policies shaved hundreds of terabytes of memory from a global resolver fleet. The change improves throughput and latency while reducing operational costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 讲解了通过重构 DNS 缓存结构和淘汰策略，在全球解析器网络中节省了数百 TB 内存。此举提升了吞吐和延迟表现，同时降低运维成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M1185AQ3B90700DKB48C2HHM.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M1185AQ3B90700DKB48C2HHM.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c1e1vg0gjl5o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US strikes $1.2B deal to pay German firm to halt offshore wind projects</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. government struck a $1.2 billion deal with a German wind turbine maker to halt offshore wind projects. The arrangement underscores how policy, regulatory risk, and financial settlements can influence the pace of renewables deployment and the economics of maritime wind development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国政府就暂停海上风电项目与德国公司达成 12 亿美元协议。此举揭示了政策、监管风险和财政安排如何影响可再生能源的推进速度及海上市风电的经济性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49332981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ysph.yale.edu/news-article/universal-health-coverage-could-save-one-trillion-dollars-and-114000-lives-every-year/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Universal health coverage could save $1T and 114k lives a year: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>耶鲁研究：全民医保每年可节省1万亿美元并挽救11.4万条生命</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49332981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 851</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49332981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 887</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49332981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Yale study argues universal health coverage could save about $1 trillion annually and prevent roughly 114,000 deaths per year. The findings offer a data-driven case for policy reforms that blend health IT with broader economic benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>耶鲁的研究显示全民医保每年或可节省约1万亿美元并挽救约11.4万条生命，为将健康 IT 与更广泛的经济效益结合的政策改革提供数据支撑。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Every Fucking Website is a 2020 project that curates a vast directory of sites, reflecting the sheer scale of the web. It serves as a provocative reference for web exploration and raises questions about discoverability in a trillion-page ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Every Fucking Website》是 2020 年的一个汇集大量网站的项目，体现了网络的庞大规模。它为网络探索提供一种挑衅性的参照，也引发了在海量站点中如何发现内容的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49421158' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49421158' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xusheng.dev/posts/reversing/mspaint_invisible_watermark/main/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MS Paint and Photos inivisibly watermark even locally generated output with GUID</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MS Paint 与 Photos 对本地生成输出隐藏水印</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49421158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 848</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49421158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49421158' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found that MS Paint and Photos embed invisible watermarks in images, even for outputs created locally, using GUIDs. The watermarking enables provenance tracking but raises privacy and control concerns for users who expect watermark-free content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，MS Paint 与 Photos 在本地生成的图片中嵌入不可见水印，使用 GUID 进行标记。水印提供可溯源性，但引发隐私与对内容控制的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xusheng.dev/posts/reversing/mspaint_invisible_watermark/social-preview.jpg' data-full='https://xusheng.dev/posts/reversing/mspaint_invisible_watermark/social-preview.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
