<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49486172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 824</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49486172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 516</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49486172' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Our decision on Cursor after SpaceX&#x27;s acquisition clarifies the product&#x27;s future and commitments to users. The plan outlines how Cursor will be supported and positioned within SpaceX&#x27;s ecosystem, with implications for developers and enterprise deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cursor 在被 SpaceX 收购后未来走向的决定，明确了对用户的承诺与支持方向。文档说明 Cursor 的未来定位、在 SpaceX 生态中的角色，以及对开发者和企业部署的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6RFKP8tysuxOLxVaiCRlu/ac31b03c2aeb6e3da620f2db89c7b6e9/index-our-decision-on-cursor-following-its-acquisition-by-spacex.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/6RFKP8tysuxOLxVaiCRlu/ac31b03c2aeb6e3da620f2db89c7b6e9/index-our-decision-on-cursor-following-its-acquisition-by-spacex.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49347543' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49347543' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.experimental-history.com/p/i-like-em-thick' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I like &#x27;em thick: an apology to my English teachers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我偏爱厚重的笔触：给英语老师的一封道歉信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49347543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 813</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49347543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49347543' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on prose density and the classroom. The author embraces a thicker, more opinionated writing style while acknowledging the critiques English teachers champion, and argues craft matters as much as correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对厚重写作风格的一次自我反思。作者在承认老师对语言的严格要求的同时，拥抱更厚实、直白的笔触，强调写作的风格与技巧同样重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KGpc!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F56ced727-423b-4a20-b330-8769c728da1c_1146x1623.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KGpc!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F56ced727-423b-4a20-b330-8769c728da1c_1146x1623.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49364745' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49364745' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117057099753905023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google has stopped pushing Git tags for some Android source code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌停止为部分 Android 源代码推送 Git 标签</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49364745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 807</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49364745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49364745' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google has stopped pushing Git tags on parts of Android&#x27;s source code, complicating reproducible builds and historical references for developers and downstream forks. The move underscores the fragility of automated versioning in large, evolving codebases and could affect security audits and CI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌不再为部分 Android 源代码推送 Git 标签，给开发者、下游分支的可复现构建与版本追溯带来挑战。这一举动凸显在大型、持续演进的代码库中自动化版本控制的脆弱性，可能影响安全审计和持续集成流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49234675' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49234675' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/articles/how-i-use-llms-to-learn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use LLMs to learn complex topics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何用大语言模型学习复杂主题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 531</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49234675' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I use a staged approach to learning with LLMs: map an outline with the model, generate concise explanations, then test understanding with targeted prompts. This turns the model into a structured tutor that surfaces gaps and helps build solid mental models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将学习分成阶段使用模型：用它勾勒大纲、生成简明解释、再通过有针对性的提问检验理解。把模型当作高效的学习辅导工具，帮助建立稳固的认知模型并暴露薄弱点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' data-full='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49433316' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49433316' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-introduces-new-mac-studio-with-m5-max-and-m5-ultra/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Mac Studio with M5 Max and M5 Ultra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果推出新款 Mac Studio，搭载 M5 Max 与 M5 Ultra</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 535</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49433316' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple expands the Mac Studio lineup with the M5 Max and M5 Ultra, pairing the compact desktop chassis with redesigned high-end chips. The new CPUs and GPUs aim to boost workstation-class performance for creators and developers, especially in AI-enabled workloads and large-scale builds. This reinforces Apple&#x27;s strategy of delivering formidable pro compute in a small form factor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在 Mac Studio 系列中引入 M5 Max 和 M5 Ultra，继续以紧凑机箱实现高端算力。新芯片对创作者与开发者的 AI 相关工作负载以及大型编译任务提供更强性能，彰显苹果在小型工作站领域提升 Pro 级算力的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-introduces-new-mac-studio-with-m5-max-and-m5-ultra/tile/Apple-Mac-Studio-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-introduces-new-mac-studio-with-m5-max-and-m5-ultra/tile/Apple-Mac-Studio-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49478178' data-tags='Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49478178' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://four.htmx.org/announcements/2026-08-28-htmx-4.0.0-is-released' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Htmx 4.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Htmx 4.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49478178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 803</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49478178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49478178' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Htmx 4.0 launches with a refreshed API surface and broader server-driven UI capabilities, further simplifying dynamic pages without heavy JavaScript frameworks. The release tightens bindings around hx- attributes and adds improvements for streaming and partial updates, keeping htmx attractive for developers seeking low-friction interactivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Htmx 4.0 发布，API 面貌焕新，扩展了服务器驱动的 UI 能力，进一步简化在不依赖大规模 JS 框架的情况下实现动态页面的开发。新版本加强了对 hx- 属性的集成，并在流式更新与局部页面更新方面带来改进，继续巩固了它在追求低摩擦交互的开发者中的定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://four.htmx.org/img/og-image.png' data-full='https://four.htmx.org/img/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49324087' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49324087' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/08/anthropics_watermark_text_adulteration_in_claude_is_a_perversion_of_writing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s ‘watermark’ text adulteration in Claude is a perversion of writing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的 Claude 水印文本被篡改：对写作的扭曲</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 797</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 697</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49324087' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Watermarking AI-produced text in Claude relies on injecting markers that distort writing, raising questions about authorial voice and readability. Critics argue this approach perverts the aesthetics and trust of AI-assisted writing, especially in creative or editorial work. The piece calls for safer, less invasive methods to attribute AI authorship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Claude 上进行文本水印时，文本被人为改动，损害了写作的可读性与作者声线。批评者认为这种做法扭曲了创作的美感和对AI辅助写作的信任，尤其在编辑和创作场景中更为突出。文章呼吁采用更安全、侵入性更小的署名方法以实现归属。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' data-full='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49479878' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49479878' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/zai-org/GLM-5.3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.3 is now open-weight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.3 已开源权重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49479878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 796</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49479878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49479878' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-weight release lets researchers run the model locally and inspect behavior; it aids reproducibility and benchmarking. The move signals growing openness in large-language-model ecosystems and invites community experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开权重意味着研究者可以本地运行模型、研究推理过程，提升复现实验和基准对比的可信度。此举体现了大语言模型生态的开放化趋势，并鼓励社区参与和改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HQ0WAnyXIAENtQA.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HQ0WAnyXIAENtQA.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49452709' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49452709' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/u-s-state-department-pauses-immigrant-visa-applications-25b31b23' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. State Department pauses immigrant visa applications</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国国务院暂停移民签证申请</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49452709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49452709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49452709' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. State Department paused immigrant visa applications amid processing backlogs and security concerns, affecting applicants and consular operations. The halt is expected to have widespread impact on families awaiting immigration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国国务院因处理积压与安全问题暂停移民签证申请，影响申请人及领事馆运作。此举预计会对等待移民的家庭造成广泛影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49411102' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49411102' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/5ee49718-c258-4f01-aa32-7e5b76ae5245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s best AI model struggles to attract users as cheaper tools thrive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 的最佳 AI 模型在吸引用户方面遇冷，便宜工具更受欢迎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49411102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 788</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49411102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 692</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49411102' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that Anthropic&#x27;s top AI model is failing to attract users as cheaper competitors gain traction, raising questions about pricing, UX, and use-case fit. The piece suggests that access cost and ease of integration matter as much as raw capability for adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示Anthropic 的顶尖 AI 模型在市场上难以吸引用户，便宜的竞争对手更具吸引力。这提出定价、用户体验和用例匹配等因素与能力同等重要的结论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Ff6698aa8-cd7a-44bb-b641-a76a0ecbe302.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Ff6698aa8-cd7a-44bb-b641-a76a0ecbe302.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49466917' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49466917' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calv.info/small-models-have-arrived' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Small Models Have Arrived</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小型模型已到来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49466917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 782</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49466917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49466917' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Smaller AI models are reaching practical viability, enabling on-device and cost-effective deployments. The piece surveys advances in distillation, quantization, and architecture design that let smaller models perform surprisingly well.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>更小的AI模型正在具备实际应用能力，能够在本地设备上部署并降低成本。文章梳理了蒸馏、量化和架构设计方面的进展，使小模型的性能出人意料地强。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://calv.info/api/og?title=Small%20Models%20Have%20Arrived' data-full='https://calv.info/api/og?title=Small%20Models%20Have%20Arrived' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49204352' data-tags='Legal,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49204352' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/aug/06/new-mexico-court-meta' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Mexico court orders Meta to pay $567m over harms to children’s mental health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 782</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204352' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New Mexico court has ordered Meta to pay $567 million after finding that its platforms harmed children’s mental health. The ruling adds to growing regulatory pressure on social media companies and could push Meta to adjust safety measures and user protections, even as enforcement costs rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人的心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元。此案加剧了对社交媒体平台在青少年福祉方面的监管压力，并可能促使 Meta 在安全措施和用户保护方面做出更多调整，监管成本也在上升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e9c72f1d4d1541ee16137214046b3255b6a1981/410_0_6827_5464/master/6827.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=bde32639cfe3328257ea8f512bc97b88' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e9c72f1d4d1541ee16137214046b3255b6a1981/410_0_6827_5464/master/6827.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=bde32639cfe3328257ea8f512bc97b88' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49495372' data-tags='Politics,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49495372' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-lawmakers-unanimously-pass-linux-exemption-from-age-verification-law-software-distributed-under-the-gpl-mit-bsd-and-apache-licenses-are-exempt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California lawmakers unanimously pass Linux exemption from age-verification law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州通过 Linux 豁免年龄验证法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49495372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49495372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 389</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49495372' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels. The move clarifies that common open-source licenses shield software from age checks while still applying to other content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。这一举措澄清了在某些内容需要年龄验证时，开源许可的软件不受这些限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G9dtD87HfBhVHzDmSHE2tE-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G9dtD87HfBhVHzDmSHE2tE-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49324985' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49324985' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Aug/16/qwen-38-27b/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8 27B is excellent, but it defaults to overthinking things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B：出色但默认易过度推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49324985' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B performs strongly, but its default prompting overemphasizes exhaustive reasoning, slowing or muddying responses. The author discusses practical tweaks to get clearer, faster outputs without sacrificing accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B 性能出色，但默认设置容易走向冗长推理，影响输出的清晰度与速度。文中给出在不牺牲准确性的前提下提高回答简洁性的实用调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/qwen-thinking-bicycle-27b.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/qwen-thinking-bicycle-27b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49167113' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-Generated Images Discourage Me from Reading Your Blog</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的图片让我不再愿意读你的博客</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 769</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49167113' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An author argues that AI-generated images reduce engagement with blog posts, making content feel artificial and distracting. The visuals can undermine the reader experience, causing skimming or distraction when images lack nuance. The piece recommends using authentic visuals—original photos or carefully curated art—to preserve reader trust and focus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为，AI 生成的图片会削弱博客的阅读体验，让内容显得不真实且分散注意力。图片若缺乏层次感可能让读者快速跳读或走神。文中建议使用真实照片或精挑细选的视觉元素来维持读者信任与专注度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/og.png' data-full='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/release-notes/system-prompts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude: System Prompts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude：系统提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 746</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 281</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319556' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude introduces new capabilities around system prompts, enabling more reliable persona control and safety constraints across conversations. The update emphasizes persistent or configurable prompts to steer model behavior without altering user prompts. This matters for developers building chatbots who require predictable, policy-compliant outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 引入了系统提示的新能力，使对话中的角色与行为更加可控和安全。更新强调可配置的系统提示，以在不改动用户提示的前提下引导模型行为。对于需要可预测、合规输出的聊天机器人开发者尤为重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepseek.com/harness/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek Harness developer preview</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek Harness 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 725</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49285244' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Harness appears to be a framework around DeepSeek AI to run experiments and benchmark performance. It could help teams standardize evaluation, compare model behavior, and improve reproducibility in AI workflows. This development comes at a time when measurable benchmarks are increasingly important for agent-powered systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Harness 似乎是围绕 DeepSeek AI 的一个用于实验运行与性能基准的框架。它有助于团队标准化评估、对比模型行为，并提升 AI 工作流的可重复性。随着对代理系统的基准衡量需求提升，这类工具的重要性也在上升。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49514878' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49514878' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Has Removed MV2 Extensions from the Chrome Web Store, Including UBO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 MV2 扩展，包括 UBO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 719</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 549</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49514878' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin. The move accelerates migration to MV3, forcing developers to update to newer APIs and potentially altering how some extensions function. This marks a significant shift in the browser extension ecosystem, with privacy tools and enterprise plugins feeling the impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。此举推动开发者尽快迁移至 MV3，可能改变部分扩展的功能与行为，并对隐私工具与企业插件生态产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49330684' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49330684' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/zkxwbgr0cnmx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident with Github.com</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Github.com 发生故障事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49330684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 718</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49330684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49330684' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub experienced a service incident that disrupted core features like repository access and pull requests. Engineers investigated, mitigated the issue, and restored normal service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 遭遇服务故障，核心功能如仓库访问和拉取请求受到影响。工程团队进行了排查、修复并恢复正常服务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49156111' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49156111' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.exe.dev/devtools-must-be-open-source' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Devtools must be open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者工具必须开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 716</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156111' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-sourcing devtools improves transparency and security through community review and faster bug-fixing. The piece argues that closed tools invite vendor lock-in and opaque risk, and outlines practical paths to open alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源开发者工具能提升透明度与安全性，因为社区评审和快速修复更易实现。文中指出封闭工具会带来供应商锁定和隐性风险，并提出让工具走向开源的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' data-full='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49154332' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49154332' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.jfrog.com/post/sqlite-critical-cves-or-llm-slops/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SQLite Critical CVEs or LLM Slop?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 关键 CVEs 或 LLM 漏洞？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 715</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154332' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises questions about SQLite&#x27;s critical CVEs, and potential LLM-related misuses, prompting readers to consider threat modeling when embedding databases with AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 SQLite 的关键 CVE 是否构成真实风险，以及与大型语言模型相关的潜在滑移/误用风险，促请在将数据库与 AI 工作流结合时进行威胁建模。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://research.jfrog.com/assets/static/jfrog-logo-svg.5788598.74a3bea875bf053c65a0663c9ec9a0fd.svg' data-full='https://research.jfrog.com/assets/static/jfrog-logo-svg.5788598.74a3bea875bf053c65a0663c9ec9a0fd.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49416055' data-tags='Startups,Business,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49416055' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modelrepublic.org/articles/a16z-portfolio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Andreessen Horowitz is investing billions into a bleak future</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>A16z在黯淡未来中投出数十亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49416055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49416055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49416055' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Andreessen Horowitz is deploying billions into bets on a purportedly bleak future, backing AI, crypto, and frontier tech across its portfolio. The piece analyzes why a16z-funded bets persist despite macro headwinds, and what it implies for startups, competition, and policy shaping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>A16z正向其投资组合投放数十亿美元，覆盖AI、加密等前沿领域，押注一个被描绘为艰难的未来。文章分析在宏观环境不确定的情况下为何仍持续进行此类长期赌注，以及这对初创公司、竞争格局与政策走向意味着什么。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/uNIrxXkbxFMlxJRgNjZm1lfmI.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=675' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/uNIrxXkbxFMlxJRgNjZm1lfmI.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49451448' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49451448' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/film/2026/aug/26/tim-curry-dies-rocky-horror-show-stephen-king-it-legend-film' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tim Curry has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蒂姆·库里去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49451448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 709</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49451448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49451448' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tim Curry, the versatile actor known for roles in The Rocky Horror Picture Show and IT, has died. Tributes highlight his flamboyant stage presence and iconic performances across film and television.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名演员蒂姆·库里去世，因其在舞台与银幕上塑造的标志性角色而广受赞誉。人们怀念他在《摇滚惊魂夜》等作品中的多才多艺与大胆表演。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/static/sys-images/Guardian/Pix/pictures/2015/11/10/1447180876442/d3b4806e-2803-42bb-8aec-690e08e5c939-2060x1236.jpeg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b04bfdbe488cdc524bb7fbaefc4933be' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/static/sys-images/Guardian/Pix/pictures/2015/11/10/1447180876442/d3b4806e-2803-42bb-8aec-690e08e5c939-2060x1236.jpeg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b04bfdbe488cdc524bb7fbaefc4933be' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49273478' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.8-2.4T</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 707</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49273478' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B is a large language model configured for 2.4 trillion parameters, optimized for inference on A95B hardware. The release underscores ongoing scaling and hardware-specific optimizations in modern LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B 是一个 2.4 万亿参数的语言模型，针对 A95B 硬件进行推理优化，凸显了当下大模型的规模化与硬件定制优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/Qwen/Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B.png' data-full='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/Qwen/Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49330781' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://duckdb.org/2026/08/17/duckdb-20-highlights' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Preview of DuckDB v2.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 2.0 预览</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49330781' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 2.0 previews show stronger analytics performance and broader SQL compatibility. The release signals a push toward production-ready analytics embedded in apps and data pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 2.0 预览展示了更强的分析性能和更广的 SQL 兼容性。此次发布表明其正向着可嵌入应用和数据管道的生产就绪分析方向发展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49478340' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.refund4freedom.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Get your Windows license refund</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>申请 Windows 许可证退款</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49478340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 706</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49478340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49478340' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many software-licensing schemes carry refund options, and this guide outlines practical steps to reclaim the cost of a Windows license. It covers common eligibility paths, expected timelines, and what documentation you’ll need to speed things up.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些 Windows 许可证购买后可以申请退款。本文提供可行的退款途径、所需材料与大致时间线，并强调在申诉时要准备好购买凭证与相关政策条款。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.refund4freedom.org/img/Asset-1.png' data-full='https://en.refund4freedom.org/img/Asset-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49510514' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49510514' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://playaphone.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Playa Phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Playa Phone：隐私优先智能手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49510514' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone is a privacy-focused smartphone project aiming to offer secure, open-source software with minimal data collection. It targets users who want more control over their data, featuring on-device processing and transparent permissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone 是一个注重隐私的智能手机项目，致力于提供安全的开源软件与极小的数据收集。该计划面向希望提升数据控制权的用户，强调本地处理与透明权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://playaphone.com/images/phone.7c2ee0b56de6.svg' data-full='https://playaphone.com/images/phone.7c2ee0b56de6.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49319633' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49319633' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.mozilla.org/en-US/kb/block-ads-firefox-ios' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox for iOS now has a native adblocker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iOS 版 Firefox 现已内置原生广告拦截器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 702</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49319633' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox iOS now ships with a native ad-blocker, improving privacy and page speed by blocking trackers and ads at the browser level. This reduces data usage and helps users bypass some invasive tracking without relying on external extensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iOS 版 Firefox 增添原生广告拦截功能，通过浏览器层面屏蔽跟踪与广告来提升隐私和页面加载速度。减少数据使用，并在无需外部扩展的情况下改善用户体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://support.mozilla.org/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' data-full='https://support.mozilla.org/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX/assets/errorIcon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49289844' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49289844' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cerebras.ai/blog/accelerating-gpt-5-6-sol-ultrafast-with-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accelerating GPT-5.6 Sol Ultrafast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加速 GPT-5.6 的 Sol Ultrafast</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49289844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49289844' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras details substantial speedups for GPT-5.6 on its Sol Ultrafast platform, combining specialized hardware with software optimizations to accelerate inference. The result underscores how hardware-software co-design can dramatically shrink response times for cutting-edge LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras 在 Sol Ultrafast 平台上为 GPT‑5.6 提供显著加速，结合定制硬件与软件优化以缩短推理时间。硬件与软件的协同设计再次推动下一代大模型的实用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/fdfac76baee0b174ea63df807f9d4f975341caea-1672x941.png?rect=0,32,1672,878&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/fdfac76baee0b174ea63df807f9d4f975341caea-1672x941.png?rect=0,32,1672,878&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49286341' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49286341' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/skitter-creek-bath-salts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spaghettifying DRAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 DRAM 拆解为意面状结构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49286341' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spaghettifying DRAM examines how unusual or chaotic memory layouts can affect latency, bandwidth, and reliability. The discussion highlights the ongoing tension between memory hardware complexity and software efficiency, suggesting approaches for hardware-aware optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spaghettifying DRAM 探讨了非传统、混乱的内存布局如何影响延迟、带宽与可靠性。该讨论凸显了内存硬件复杂性与软件效率之间的矛盾，并提出面向硬件的优化思路。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49387497' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A grand jury declined to indict a man from Ohio who was charged with destroying a Flock camera. The decision underscores the complexities of defacing surveillance tech and how prosecutors pursue such incidents. It may influence debates over private CCTV networks and public accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大陪审团决定不对一名俄亥俄州男子因毁坏Flock摄像头而提出指控。此判决凸显了破坏监控设备相关指控的难点，以及检方在此类案件中的取证与定性问题。或影响对私人监控网络与公共问责的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://timmarinin.net/2026/bluesky-screenshots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Bluesky draws its logo on screenshots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bluesky 如何在截屏上绘制其标志</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49338459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49338459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 434</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49338459' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bluesky overlays its logo onto screenshots, a design choice that brands shared content but can affect user experience. The piece breaks down how this watermarking works and what it implies for privacy and UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bluesky 在截屏上覆盖其标志作为品牌标识，这个设计选择影响了共享内容的用户体验。文中解释了 watermark 的实现方式及其对隐私和 UX 的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49424606' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c62m4gpnp78o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oceans hit highest temperature on record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海洋温度达到历史新高</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49424606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49424606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49424606' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oceans have reached record warm temperatures, underscoring the persistent impact of climate change. The heat uptake reshapes marine ecosystems and intensifies weather extremes, with broad implications for policy, fisheries, and carbon cycling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海洋温度达到历史最高水平，凸显气候变化的持续影响。海水升温改变海洋生态、加剧极端天气，并对政策、渔业和碳循环产生广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6e1c/live/bc7158a0-9fc3-11f1-8648-8d7b77845e6d.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6e1c/live/bc7158a0-9fc3-11f1-8648-8d7b77845e6d.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases a firmware update for the V4 Flash platform, focusing on stability, performance, and new features for data indexing and retrieval. The update underscores ongoing optimization of flash-based search systems and developer tooling.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.8-flash-next' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.8-Flash-Next</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen unveils 3.8-Flash-Next with a new architecture designed to slash total cost of ownership. Early signals point to modular design and hardware-aware optimizations that improve throughput per dollar. If adopted widely, it could shift how enterprises balance model capability with operating expense.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 推出 3.8-Flash-Next，采用新架构以显著降低总体拥有成本。初步迹象显示将采用模块化设计和面向硬件的优化提高性价比。如果被广泛采用，可能改变企业在模型能力与运维成本之间的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49257876' data-tags='Security,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49257876' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stolen-thoughts.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stealing Reasoning Traces from Proprietary LLM APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从专有大语言模型 API 窃取推理痕迹</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49257876' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that proprietary LLM APIs can leak traces of reasoning, exposing intermediate prompts and steps. This raises privacy and security concerns about using hosted models and underscores the need for defenses against leakage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了专有大语言模型 API 可能泄露推理痕迹，暴露中间提示和推理步骤。这对使用托管模型的隐私与安全构成挑战，凸显防止信息泄露的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stolen-thoughts.com/assets/fig1-extraction-fidelity.svg' data-full='https://stolen-thoughts.com/assets/fig1-extraction-fidelity.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49409073' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ericpardee.github.io/fire-hd-ownership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I spent $266 and four AI models to own my tablet. GLM-5.3 finished it in a day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我花266美元和四个AI模型搞定我的平板，GLM-5.3一天就完成了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49409073' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An enthusiast spent 266 dollars across four AI models to tackle a tablet task, with GLM-5.3 emerging as the fastest solution, finishing the job in a day. The experiment underscores how open-weight models can rapidly displace more expensive, closed options for hands-on device tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位爱好者用266美元、并借助四个AI模型完成对平板的某项挑战，结果由GLM-5.3在一天内解决。此举凸显开源权重模型在成本和速度上的竞争力，适合个人或小团队进行硬件与软件的快速试验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ericpardee.github.io/fire-hd-ownership/blog-assets/kimi-we-found-a-working-bug.png' data-full='https://ericpardee.github.io/fire-hd-ownership/blog-assets/kimi-we-found-a-working-bug.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49167448' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://birchtree.me/blog/xbox-goes-down-you-cant-play-games-you-own-on-disc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xbox goes down. You can&#x27;t play games you own on disc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xbox 系统宕机致无法离线玩光盘游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 725</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49167448' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outage during which Xbox users cannot play disc-owned games highlights how modern consoles lean on online services even for physical media. The incident underscores resilience risks, DRM entanglements, and the trade-offs of cloud-enabled ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xbox 的服务中断让玩家即便拥有光盘游戏也无法离线游玩，凸显现代主机生态对在线服务的依赖。事件暴露了系统韧性、数字版权管理与“拥有即用”的取舍之间的矛盾。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49517448' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify promises a leaner, faster Spotify-like experience, focusing on lower latency and a smaller client footprint. If it delivers, it could push streaming tooling toward lighter, more open architectures and raise questions about compatibility and licensing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify 提出更轻量、快速的 Spotify 风格体验，聚焦更低的延迟和更小的客户端体积。如果落地，它可能推动流媒体工具走向更开放、轻量化的架构，并带来兼容性与许可方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fastpotify.rocks/screenshot.png' data-full='https://fastpotify.rocks/screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An eclectic reading list contrasts crime-focused topics with botany, using the tension between risk and growth to illustrate how different domains reward careful inquiry and robust systems thinking. It offers cross-disciplinary insights into detection, growth, and learning strategies valuable to technologists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份以犯罪题材与植物学之间的对比为主的阅读清单，揭示不同领域对好奇心、方法论和系统思维的奖励机制。通过跨学科的对照，帮助科技工作者理解数据模式、风险评估与学习策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49387570' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49387570' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lina.sh/blog/hijacking-e164-arpa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I accidentally logged hundreds of thousands of phone calls to military bases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不小心把数十万通电话记录到军事基地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49387570' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blogger reveals that misconfigured telecom routing accidentally logged hundreds of thousands of calls destined for military bases. The incident highlights telecom privacy risks and the potential for misrouting to expose sensitive endpoints. It raises questions about how edge-case routing bugs are monitored and mitigated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博主披露，由于配置或路由错误，数十万通电话被错误路由记录指向军事基地。此事凸显电信隐私风险与错误路由对敏感终端的潜在影响，也引发对边缘路由漏洞的监控与缓解的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lina.sh/assets/blog/e164/cover.png' data-full='https://lina.sh/assets/blog/e164/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49239751' data-tags='DevOps,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49239751' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.docker.com/products/docker-sandboxes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docker Sandboxes – Disposable, isolated sandboxes for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docker 沙盒：面向 AI 代理的一次性隔离沙箱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 663</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49239751' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docker Sandboxes provide disposable, isolated environments for running AI agents, enabling safer experimentation and reproducibility. They offer controlled dependencies and snapshot-like configurations to prevent cross-agent interference. This helps teams prototype AI tasks without risking the host system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docker Sandboxes 提供一次性、隔离的运行环境，专为 AI 代理设计，降低实验风险并提高可重复性。通过可控依赖和快照式配置，开发者可以在不影响宿主系统的情况下安全试验复杂的 AI 任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.docker.com/app/uploads/2024/02/docker-default-meta-image-1110x583.png' data-full='https://www.docker.com/app/uploads/2024/02/docker-default-meta-image-1110x583.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49182996' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49182996' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-os/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare OS: an open platform for agents, apps, and work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare OS：面向代理、应用与工作的新开放平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 658</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49182996' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare unveils Cloudflare OS, an open platform designed to host agents, apps, and work workflows at the edge. It aims to broaden extensibility across Cloudflare&#x27;s edge network by letting developers deploy自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出 Cloudflare OS，这是一个面向在边缘运行的代理、应用和工作流的开放平台。通过允许开发者部署自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ7PN06AYFEQJN4FBVAE8WHH.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ7PN06AYFEQJN4FBVAE8WHH.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49126604' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49126604' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yc-software/qm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>qm – Multiplayer agent harness for work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>qm：面向工作场景的多人代理框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49126604' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个现代化、交互式的机械连杆目录，展示了507种不同的运动方式。为设计师和工程师提供直观的可视参考，帮助理解各部件如何转换运动。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notebookcheck.net/Windows-11-s-built-in-Weather-app-wastes-more-than-1-GB-of-RAM.1364205.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11&#x27;s built-in Weather app wastes more than 1 GB of RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 内置天气应用耗费超过 1GB RAM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 653</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232138' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A review finds the built-in Weather app consumes over a gigabyte of RAM, highlighting how preinstalled apps can bloat systems and affect performance even on modern hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评测发现 Windows 11 内置天气应用的内存占用超过 1GB，凸显系统自带应用的资源占用及对性能的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/Windows_11_01.jpg' data-full='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/Windows_11_01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49251411' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49251411' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.effort.news/uk-lobby' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The UK&#x27;s war on anonymity has come to America</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国对匿名性的打压蔓延至美国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49251411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49251411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 748</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49251411' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A UK-style push to curb online anonymity is gaining traction in the US, with lawmakers favoring identity verification and traceability. The move signals a broader shift toward user accountability that could reshape platform moderation, data sharing, and civil liberties. For technologists, the takeaway is that privacy-centered design may need stronger defaults and clearer opt-outs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国对匿名性的打压正在美国获得关注，立法者日益支持身份核验和可追溯性。此举预示着向用户问责的全球性潮流，可能影响平台治理、数据共享和公民自由。对技术人来说，隐私友好设计需要更强的默认保护与清晰的选择权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.effort.news/images/article-graphics/uk-lobby/reset-bipartite-graph-cover.png' data-full='https://www.effort.news/images/article-graphics/uk-lobby/reset-bipartite-graph-cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49263497' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49263497' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ngrok.com/blog/compression-is-prediction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compression is prediction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>压缩即预测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 651</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49263497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49263497' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Data compression is fundamentally about predicting the next symbol; improving a compressor equates to a better predictor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据压缩本质上是对下一个符号的预测；提升压缩效果等同于改进预测能力。这一观点对机器学习、数据存储与推理具有实际启示，适用于希望把压缩思路融入模型设计的开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-11-compression-is-prediction/og.png' data-full='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-11-compression-is-prediction/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49390427' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49390427' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bandarlabs.github.io/Cobalt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kobo can run apps now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kobo 电子书阅读器现在可以运行应用程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49390427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 647</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49390427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49390427' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kobo devices now support running apps through a new runtime, expanding their functionality beyond ebooks. This brings on-device tools and services closer to rival ecosystems, potentially changing how users interact with e-readers. For developers, it opens a new platform with modest hardware requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kobo 电子书阅读器现在可以在设备上运行应用程序，扩展了其功能。此举将对设备生态带来新的工具与服务，改变用户使用电子书阅读器的方式；对开发者而言，意味着在相对低配硬件上进入新平台的机会。</p>

</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49452990' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49452990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailcat – Like netcat, but over Tailscale’s data plane</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailcat：在 Tailscale 数据平面上的 netcat 替代品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49452990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 644</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49452990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49452990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tailcat provides a netcat-style data channel that rides on Tailscale&#x27;s data plane, allowing direct, authenticated connections between devices regardless of NATs. It offers a lightweight way to test services, tunnel traffic, and diagnose network issues without exposing endpoints publicly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tailcat 在 Tailscale 的数据平面上实现类似 netcat 的数据通道，能够在 NAT 穿透的设备之间建立经过认证的直接连接。它为测试、隧道传输和网络排错提供了一个轻量的替代方案，无需暴露公网端点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c7a50530474274f26cbe881658811716332fdf6d179ad5a2de2d40854000b9be/tailscale/tailcat' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c7a50530474274f26cbe881658811716332fdf6d179ad5a2de2d40854000b9be/tailscale/tailcat' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
