<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mezha.net/eng/bukvy/ca117584_denmark_requires_oral/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Danish high schoolers will have to verbally defend written assignments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦高中生将被要求口头辩护书面作业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49224294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49224294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49224294' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Denmark will require students to orally defend their written assignments, adding an extra dimension to assessment and reducing potential biases. The move has implications for exam infrastructure and remote/AI-assisted evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丹麦新规要求学生对书面作业进行口头辩护，增加评估的维度并降低偏见。这对考试基础设施和在线/AI 辅助评估提出新挑战与需求。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgvyy4x2mvo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canada will match US tariffs &#x27;dollar for dollar&#x27; as trade talks break down</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49397074' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada vows reciprocal tariffs if talks fail, signaling a tougher trade posture that could ripple through tech supply chains and consumer electronics pricing across North America. The move could impact electronics pricing and cross-border commerce more broadly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大承诺在与美方谈判破裂时对美关税进行对等报复，预示跨境贸易环境将趋于强硬，可能影响北美地区的科技供应链与消费电子定价。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/4e3957f8-ea7c-4c46-a3de-cdce8e526878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mark Zuckerberg attacks &#x27;closed&#x27; AI rivals as Meta returns to open models</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49243880' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mark Zuckerberg frames Meta’s pivot toward open-weight AI models as a stance against closed rivals, signaling a push for open standards and modular AI. The move underscores the industry tension between proprietary systems and open collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马克·扎克伯格将 Meta 的策略定位为对抗封闭式 AI 竞争对手，推动采用开放权重模型。此举体现了对开放标准与模块化 AI 的重视，以及行业在开放与封闭之间的博弈。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存价格在一年内上涨 500%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49334960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 634</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49334960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 513</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49334960' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memory prices surged 500% year over year, with high-end DDR5 kits reaching thousands of dollars per kit; supply-chain pressures and demand for AI workloads drive the spike. The article warns about the impact on builders and data centers, and signals potential normalization after inventory adjustments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内存价格在一年内暴涨约 500%，高端 DDR5 套件价格甚至达到数千美元。供应链压力和对 AI 工作负载的需求推动价格上涨，文章分析对硬件建设者与数据中心的影响，并暗示库存回稳后可能回落。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49395628' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/perf-opt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There&#x27;s no reason for software to be slow anymore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件再也没有慢的理由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49395628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49395628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 484</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49395628' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that with modern hardware, tooling, and architecture, software can be fast without sacrificing developer productivity. It outlines pragmatic approaches to eliminate unnecessary latency in everyday apps. The main takeaway is that performance should be a default, not an afterthought.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为借助现代硬件、工具链和架构，软件可以变得更快且不妥协开发效率。它提出实用的做法来消除日常应用中的不必要延迟，强调性能应成为默认目标而非事后考虑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49362934' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49362934' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.colorado.edu/today/2026/08/12/remote-workers-report-highest-well-being-study-7700-employees' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Remote workers report the highest well-being in study of 7,700 employees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 7,700 名员工的研究中，远程工作者幸福感最高</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 632</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49362934' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Colorado study of 7,700 workers finds remote employees report the highest well-being, with in-office workers faring worse on several metrics. The results support flexible work policies, but context matters for productivity and inclusion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 7,700 名员工的研究中，远程工作者的幸福感最高，在职场幸福感等指标上相较于在岗员工表现更好。研究支持灵活的工作政策，但生产力与包容性取决于具体执行情境。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49322107' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49322107' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Cloudflare silently injects its analytics when you switch nameservers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN: Cloudflare 在切换域名服务器时悄然注入分析代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322107' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49322107' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post alleges Cloudflare injects its analytics code when you switch DNS, enabling traffic telemetry without explicit consent. The finding raises questions about privacy, transparency, and the trade-offs of using a privacy-focused CDN.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人指称 Cloudflare 在你切换 DNS 时悄然注入分析代码，进行流量遥测而未征得明确同意。这一发现引发对隐私、透明度及使用隐私导向 CDNs 时权衡取舍的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49378957' data-tags='Web,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49378957' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/the-august-17-outage-and-the-work-ahead/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The August 17 outage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>8月17日的宕机事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 744</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49378957' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The outage on August 17 disrupted services and prompted a review of incident response and reliability practices. Leadership outlines steps to improve observability, recovery, and post-mortem processes to prevent future outages. The episode underscores how even major platforms must invest in resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>8月17日的宕机影响广泛，促使团队回顾事件响应与可用性实践。负责人列出提升可观测性、恢复能力和事后分析的措施，以防止类似故障再次发生。这一事件凸显了大型平台在韧性建设上的持续投入必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-left.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-left.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49273165' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49273165' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewpwheeler.com/2026/08/12/license-plate-reader-searches-should-require-a-warrant/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>License plate reader searches should require a warrant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>车牌识别搜索应当获得授权才可进行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49273165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49273165' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy arguments are made for requiring warrants to search license plate data collected by LPR systems. It argues targeted, lawfully justified access over generic bulk surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在使用车牌识别（LPR）系统检索数据时应当获得搜查令的政策主张。文章强调公民自由与滥用风险、数据保留与共享的隐私边界，以及应优先实现有针对性且合法授权的访问，而非大规模监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andrewpwheeler.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/apwface.png?w=200' data-full='https://andrewpwheeler.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/06/apwface.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://x.ai/news/grok-4-6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok 4.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 4.6</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49274027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49274027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49274027' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 updates the AI assistant with refinements to conversational ability, integration options, and developer-facing tooling. The release highlights ongoing efforts to evolve AI assistants into more capable, context-aware productivity partners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.6 对AI助手进行了若干改进，提升对话能力并扩展集成与开发工具。此次发布凸显了让AI助手成为更具情境感知的高效生产力伙伴的持续努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://x.ai/images/news/grok-4-6-og.png' data-full='https://x.ai/images/news/grok-4-6-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49337602' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49337602' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrouter.ai/openai/gpt-5.6-sol' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Sol Pricing Cut by 50% on OpenRouter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol 价格下调 50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49337602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49337602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49337602' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI-compatible GPT-5.6 Sol has its pricing cut by half, making inference and experimentation cheaper for developers. The move could accelerate adoption in cost-sensitive AI workloads and broaden the competitive landscape for AI runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol 的价格下降了 50%，让开发者在推理和试验阶段成本更低。这一举措可能推动低成本 AI 工作负载的采用，并改变 AI 运行时的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openrouter.ai/openai/gpt-5.6-sol/opengraph-image-1oizug?abd1ffaa6102aa5f' data-full='https://openrouter.ai/openai/gpt-5.6-sol/opengraph-image-1oizug?abd1ffaa6102aa5f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49136821' data-tags='Web,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49136821' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrss.org/blog/how-google-helped-destroy-adoption-of-rss-feeds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Google helped destroy adoption of RSS feeds (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌如何助推RSS订阅的衰落（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 623</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49136821' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google’s product and policy choices helped marginalize RSS, accelerating its decline in adoption. Centralized platforms controlled feed discovery and access, leaving RSS ecosystems with limited visibility; for builders, the takeaway is to rely on open standards and independent readers rather than platform-dictated discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾谷歌的一系列产品与策略如何弱化RSS的普及，推动其走向边缘。平台对内容发现的垄断让RSS缺乏可见度，开发者应坚持开放标准与独立阅读器，以避免被平台支配的发现机制所束缚。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openrss.orghttps://openrss.org/media/google-links-break.jpg' data-full='https://openrss.orghttps://openrss.org/media/google-links-break.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49242739' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49242739' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bobdahacker.com/blog/tldv-hack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tl;dv: Over 180k meetings left wide open</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tl;dv：超过18万次会议被暴露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49242739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49242739' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tl;dv reveals that over 180,000 meetings were left exposed due to misconfigured access controls, raising concerns about the privacy of meeting transcripts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tl;dv 指出有超过 18 万场会议因访问控制配置不当而暴露，凸显会议记录隐私的潜在风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/tldv/blogHeader.png' data-full='https://bobdahacker.com/static/images/blogs/tldv/blogHeader.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49312845' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49312845' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidepiffer.com/p/ai-isnt-outthinking-mathematicians' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI isn’t outthinking mathematicians, it’s out-remembering them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI并非在推理上胜过数学家，而是在记忆力上胜过他们</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 620</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49312845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 495</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49312845' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI&#x27;s edge in math comes from vast memory and retrieval rather than genuine reasoning. It can assemble and recall many proofs or results quickly, enabling rapid exploration but still faltering on truly new problems. For practitioners, the lesson is to design workflows that leverage AI for recall and verification, not rely on it for original mathematical discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI在数学领域的优势来自于海量的记忆与检索能力，而非真正的推理。它能快速汇集和回忆大量证明和结果，但在解决全新难题上仍会吃瘪。对工程师而言，关键在于把AI当作记忆与验证的工具，而不是原创数学发现的源泉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DIxw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2e2e14e7-c536-49c0-9f27-79b1073dd527_1279x759.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DIxw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2e2e14e7-c536-49c0-9f27-79b1073dd527_1279x759.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49157930' data-tags='Science,Programming,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49157930' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/ten-advances-in-mathematics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ten advances in mathematics and theoretical computer science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学与理论计算机科学的十项进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 920</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157930' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A survey of ten notable advances at the mathematics-theory CS frontier, spanning new results in algorithmic information theory, cryptography, and mathematical methods. The core takeaway is that foundational research continues to drive practical gains in security, verification, and AI theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文盘点数学与理论计算机科学前沿的十项进展，涵盖算法信息理论、密码学和新数学方法等领域。核心在于基础研究持续推动安全、验证与人工智能理论等实际应用的突破。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5RUlCTeEvzWYd2DDpdcNgh/f38e144bf6f5911074b1939c1528ed2b/Frame__4_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5RUlCTeEvzWYd2DDpdcNgh/f38e144bf6f5911074b1939c1528ed2b/Frame__4_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49351324' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49351324' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://about.iceland.co.uk/our-story/the-dark-ages/beware-management-consultants/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beware Management Consultants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>當心管理顧問</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 613</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49351324' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Management consultants can deliver frameworks and quick fixes, but they also risk imposing one‑size‑fits‑all solutions, high fees, and disruptions that hollow out internal capability. Skepticism and clear metrics are needed before outsourcing strategy or transformation work to avoid short‑term gains at the expense of long‑term resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>管理顧問雖能提供框架與速效方案，但也可能強行套用通用解法、收取高額費用並削弱內部能力。對外包策略或轉型工作應保持懷疑、設定明確指標，以免短期收益代價是長期抗壓力下降。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://about.iceland.co.uk/wp-content/uploads/2017/11/logo-1.png' data-full='https://about.iceland.co.uk/wp-content/uploads/2017/11/logo-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49170165' data-tags='Show HN,Design,Data,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49170165' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://toneyalexander.github.io/inclusive-color-space/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Simple algorithm and color space to generate diverse skin tones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用简单算法和色彩空间生成多样肤色</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49170165' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight method enables generating diverse skin tones using a simple algorithm and color space. This approach helps create more inclusive avatars, datasets, and UI themes by reducing bias in representation. It demonstrates how principled color-space choices can improve realism without heavy machine learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个轻量方法通过简单算法和色彩空间实现多样肤色，便于在头像、数据集和界面中提供更包容的色调。此类方法有助于减少表现偏差，提升真实感，且不需要复杂的模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://toneyalexander.github.io/inclusive-color-space/img/preview.png' data-full='https://toneyalexander.github.io/inclusive-color-space/img/preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49321717' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49321717' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rvembedded.com/blog_post/12/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A third world engineer responds to “RISC-V: They should have known better”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自第三世界的工程师回应“RISC-V：他们本应更懂行”的质疑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49321717' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software/embedded engineer from a developing country pushes back against a dismissive claim that RISC-V developers should have known better, arguing that open ISAs democratize hardware innovation and reduce dependence on entrenched vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位来自发展中国家的工程师反驳对 RISC-V 开发者应更知情的轻视论调，认为开放指令集民主化硬件创新，降低对传统厂商的依赖。文中强调资金、供应链与教育等现实约束其实可能催生符合本地条件的务实进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rvembedded.com/static/main/images/rovari_logo_color_128w.png' data-full='https://rvembedded.com/static/main/images/rovari_logo_color_128w.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49270726' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://digitalescapetools.com/2026/08/ublock-origin-stops-chasing-facebook-ads.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>uBlock Origin Is Giving Up the Fight to Keep Ads Off Facebook</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>uBlock Origin 放弃对 Facebook 广告的拦截</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49270726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 726</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49270726' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>uBlock Origin reportedly stops fighting Facebook ads as Facebook arms itself with anti-adblock techniques. The move shifts the burden to site owners and raises questions about the future of comprehensive ad blocking on social networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>称为 Facebook 引入对抗广告拦截措施后，uBlock Origin 不再继续拦截 Facebook 广告。此举将拦截责任转向站点所有者，并引发对社交平台上全面广告屏蔽未来的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117078064184215730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Devices with GrapheneOS support should be available in 2027</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49360242' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Devices with GrapheneOS support are projected to ship in 2027, signaling a broader push for privacy-focused smartphones. The timeline hints at partnerships and hardware support expanding beyond a niche audience, with implications for security-minded consumers and device makers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预计 2027 年将有支持 GrapheneOS 的设备上市，表明隐私优先手机将进入更广阔市场。时间表暗示硬件伙伴关系与生态扩张，将影响关注安全的消费者与设备厂商。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49127306' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/hugging-face-intrusion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale didn&#x27;t stop the Hugging Face intrusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale 未阻止 Hugging Face 入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49127306' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face disclosed a security incident in which attackers bypassed protections despite Tailscale, underscoring that zero-trust networking is not a sole defense. The post details how leaked credentials enabled access and the lessons about defense-in-depth and secret hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face 公开了一起安全事件，攻击者在使用 Tailscale 的情况下仍然进入内部系统，说明零信任网络并非万用钥匙。文中描述了凭证泄露如何促成入侵，并强调需要多层防护与良好密钥管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=0dafcba5-55df-4373-be5e-564b368a27af' data-full='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=0dafcba5-55df-4373-be5e-564b368a27af' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/news/?id=1ptvdtcm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iCloud+ Hide My Email addresses will remain on icloud.com</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49426564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49426564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49426564' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple says Hide My Email aliases will stay on iCloud.com, ensuring consistent privacy options across devices. The move affects how users manage aliases, inbox organization, and service interoperability within Apple’s ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果表示 Hide My Email 的邮箱别名将继续保留在 iCloud.com 上，确保跨设备的隐私选项一致。此举影响用户如何管理别名、收件箱组织以及在苹果生态系统内的服务互操作性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个数据驱动的可视化把旧金山想象成一个电子游戏，提供对城市的互动式探索。该项目展示了城市数据与地图如何孕育新型体验，并可能为城市规划与无障碍设计提供新视角。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49473522' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/08/27/technology/anthropic-government-blacklisting-ruling.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge rules Trump administration’s blacklisting of Anthropic was illegal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官裁定特朗普政府对 Anthropic 的黑名单措施违法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49473522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 596</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49473522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49473522' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court found that the Trump administration&#x27;s blacklisting of Anthropic was illegal, citing improper authority or process. The ruling reinforces legal checks on government actions in tech and procurement, and may influence future regulatory tactics in the AI ecosystem. The decision signals enforceable limits on executive power in tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定特朗普政府试图将 Anthropic 列入黑名单的行为违法，指出权力与程序受限。判决强调政府在科技采购中的权力边界与法治约束，可能影响未来对 AI 公司的监管手段，对政府打击科技公司的策略形成制衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49361395' data-tags='Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49361395' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/NoubarAfeyan/status/2090050162441752787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moderna reports first positive Phase 3 for mRNA neoantigen therapy in melanoma</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moderna 就黑色素瘤的 mRNA 新抗原治疗进入三期首获积极结果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49361395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 592</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49361395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49361395' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moderna reports the first positive Phase 3 result for an mRNA neoantigen therapy in melanoma, signaling a potential breakthrough in personalized cancer vaccines. While encouraging, further trials and regulatory review are needed before clinical use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moderna 就黑色素瘤的 mRNA 新抗原治疗公布首例三期阳性结果，代表个性化癌症疫苗领域的潜在突破。前景乐观，但仍需更多试验与监管评估以实现临床应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1547613569734156289/GetowkOg_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1547613569734156289/GetowkOg_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49343559' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49343559' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/ai-and-ml/2026/08/18/google-buys-crashed-airline-spirits-data-at-auction-because-ai/5288962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google has acquired the data of failed US airline Spirit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌買下倒閉航空公司的資料</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49343559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49343559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 408</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49343559' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google reportedly purchased operational and passenger data from a failed U.S. airline at auction, potentially to train models or analyze transportation systems. That acquisition raises privacy and legal questions about using sensitive passenger records for AI research or commercial products without clear consent or protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>據稱谷歌在破產拍賣中買下了一家倒閉美國航空公司的運營與乘客資料，可能用於模型訓練或運輸分析。這種做法帶出隱私與法律問題：敏感乘客紀錄被用於 AI 或商業用途時，是否具有明確同意與適當保護。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5273664.jpg?imageId=5273664&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5273664.jpg?imageId=5273664&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49373456' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simedw.com/2026/08/20/midi-autocomplete/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I trained a 125M model to autocomplete piano on-device</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在本地设备上训练的125M参数钢琴自动完成模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49373456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49373456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49373456' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 125M-parameter model trained to autocomplete piano MIDI on-device demonstrates that lightweight models can run offline with privacy-preserving capabilities. The project highlights practical design choices for on-device AI, including quantization and streaming generation, underscoring a path beyond cloud-only inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份 125M 参数的钢琴 MIDI 自动完成模型在本地设备上运行，证明轻量级模型也能离线、保护隐私。项目展示了对设备端 AI 的实际设计取舍，如量化、流式生成，为离线推理提供了可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://simedw.com/2026/08/20/midi-autocomplete/images/rolltab_phone.jpg' data-full='https://simedw.com/2026/08/20/midi-autocomplete/images/rolltab_phone.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49383026' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.gl/blog/physical-destruction.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies destroy physical books – let&#x27;s scan rare books before it&#x27;s too late</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司毁灭实体书——应及早扫描珍贵书籍以保存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49383026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49383026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 861</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49383026' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that AI-driven digitization programs risk destroying physical books, calling for urgent scanning of rare volumes to preserve knowledge before they disappear. It discusses trade-offs between preservation, access, and the biases embedded in digitization efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中主张 AI 驱动的数字化计划可能消灭实体书，呼吁尽快对稀有书籍进行数字化以保存知识。并探讨保存、获取和数字化过程中的偏见与权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://annas-archive.gl/blog/duxiu-examples/1.jpg' data-full='https://annas-archive.gl/blog/duxiu-examples/1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openshot.org/blog/2026/08/30/openshot-40-record-edit-color-like-never-before/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenShot 4.0 – Open-source video editor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenShot 4.0：开源视频编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49507822' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenShot 4.0 introduces major UI and performance improvements, with enhanced recording, editing, and color tools. The update strengthens OpenShot as a beginner-friendly, cross-platform editor and demonstrates sustained, community-driven development in open-source video tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenShot 4.0 在界面与性能方面带来显著改进，新增录制、编辑和色彩工具。此次更新巩固了它作为面向初学者、跨平台的编辑器的定位，同时体现了开源视频工具领域的社区驱动持续发展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49412396' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/paulg/status/2091544343589060625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I were 17, I&#x27;d learn how to build LLMs from scratch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果我17岁，我会从零开始学习如何构建大语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49412396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 577</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49412396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 648</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49412396' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Learning the fundamentals now pays off later. Hands-on practice building LLMs from scratch builds intuition about architectures, data, and training dynamics. The takeaway is to focus on practical experimentation rather than relying solely on prebuilt APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果年轻时就动手学习并从零开始构建LLMs，能更好掌握原理和局限。文章强调要认真理解架构、数据与训练过程，而不仅仅依赖现成的API。如此能培养工程直觉与创新能力。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 575</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49511856' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY project repurposes home security cameras to feed BirdNET-Go for automatic bird identification. The approach shows how consumer hardware can enable wildlife monitoring at scale, with low cost and ease of deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 DIY 项目将家用安防摄像头接入 BirdNET-Go，实现自动鸟类识别。此做法展示了消费级硬件在野生生物监测中的可扩展性和低成本潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49334209' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49334209' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cursor.com/changelog/origin-code-hosting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cursor launches Origin, GitHub alternative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cursor 推出 Origin：GitHub 替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49334209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49334209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49334209' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cursor launched Origin, a code‑hosting service positioned as an alternative to traditional platforms by integrating collaborative editing, code intelligence, and hosting into one product. The move targets developers who want tighter editor‑to‑host workflows and built‑in tooling instead of stitching multiple services together.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cursor 推出 Origin，將協作編輯、程式碼智能與代碼託管整合為一體，作為傳統代碼平台的替代選擇。這針對希望編輯器與託管有更緊密整合、並內建工具鏈而不想拼接多個服務的開發者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/og/og-origin-code-hosting-08-17-26-iibBEcxTigpnjXaBULY8Du327sx4P1.png' data-full='https://ptht05hbb1ssoooe.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/assets/og/og-origin-code-hosting-08-17-26-iibBEcxTigpnjXaBULY8Du327sx4P1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49411643' data-tags='Career,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49411643' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/post/find-problems-staff-engineer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I find problems to solve as a staff engineer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在担任资深工程师时如何发现待解决的问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49411643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 561</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49411643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49411643' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Learn a practical framework for identifying high-leverage problems as a staff engineer by combining system signals, user pain points, and business impact. The piece emphasizes partnering with product and ops, triaging by impact and effort, and turning findings into scoped initiatives with measurable outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍在担任资深工程师时如何通过系统信号、用户痛点和业务影响来发现高杠杆的问题，提供实用框架。强调与产品和运维协作、按影响与投入进行优先级排序，并将发现转化为有明确可衡量目标的计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lalitm.com/social-cards/find-problems-staff-engineer.png' data-full='https://lalitm.com/social-cards/find-problems-staff-engineer.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49257377' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49257377' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c75gk620r22o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>England set to be one of the first countries to eliminate hepatitis C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英格兰有望成为首批根除丙型肝炎的国家之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49257377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 400</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49257377' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>England is poised to become one of the first countries to eliminate hepatitis C, a milestone driven by broad screening and highly effective antiviral treatments. If achieved, it would validate scalable public-health strategies and could influence targets for other nations facing HCV challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英格兰有望成为首批根除丙型肝炎的国家之一，这一成就依赖广泛筛查和高效抗病毒治疗的组合。若实现，将验证可复制的公共卫生策略，并可能激励其他国家设定相似目标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f1b4/live/9d52a710-94e4-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f1b4/live/9d52a710-94e4-11f1-a7ab-8b30adf0800a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49520022' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49520022' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AnkiDroid: Google Play no longer allowing Open Collective donation link</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AnkiDroid：Google Play 不再允许 Open Collective 捐赠链接</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49520022' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Play no longer allows apps to link to external donation pages such as Open Collective, forcing AnkiDroid to remove its donation link. This policy shift illustrates how app-store rules can constrain OSS funding models and push developers toward alternative monetization or sponsorship approaches. For a long-running open-source Android project, sustaining funding remains a critical challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Play 现在不再允许应用链接到外部捐赠页面，AnkiDroid 团队因此移除了 Open Collective 的捐赠链接。这一政策变化凸显了应用商店规则对开源资金模式的制约，可能促使开发者转向应用内购买或平台赞助等替代模式。对于长期运营的开源 Android 项目来说，资金筹措仍然是关键挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3f485d6c65f61fa587b2d9b6bd6f110db71340239f3f67052e44cb9f97325143/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3f485d6c65f61fa587b2d9b6bd6f110db71340239f3f67052e44cb9f97325143/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49449576' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49449576' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitterwebviewer.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twitter Viewer – View Twitter Without Account</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Twitter Viewer：无需账户查看 Twitter</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49449576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49449576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49449576' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Twitter Viewer lets you read public tweets without signing in, offering a privacy-friendly way to access content. It can be handy for researchers, journalists, and developers, but may raise policy and data-access questions as platforms tighten APIs. It exemplifies how third-party tooling can bypass friction while highlighting the limits of platform control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Twitter Viewer 允许在不登录的情况下浏览公开推文，为访问信息提供了更隐私友好的方式。对研究人员、记者和开发者颇有帮助，但随着平台收紧 API，相关的政策与数据访问问题亦随之浮现。此工具体现了第三方工具在降低门槛与平台控制能力之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://twitterwebviewer.com/api/og?type=home&amp;title=Twitter%20Viewer&amp;subtitle=View%20Twitter%20Without%20Account' data-full='https://twitterwebviewer.com/api/og?type=home&amp;title=Twitter%20Viewer&amp;subtitle=View%20Twitter%20Without%20Account' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49421554' data-tags='AI,Education,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49421554' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://larsfaye.com/articles/ai-coding-will-prevent-expertise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coding expertise is going to collapse from AI reliance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 依赖将让编码技能崩塌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49421554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49421554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49421554' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-powered coding tools risk eroding developers&#x27; fundamental expertise if people rely on them for all reasoning. The piece argues for training models, better education, and tooling that preserve understanding of underlying concepts rather than turning programmers into button-pushers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果人们把所有推理都交给 AI 编码工具，开发者的基础专业能力可能会退化。文章主张通过改进教育、培养培训模式，以及提供能保留对根本概念理解的工具，而非让程序员成为“点按按钮者”。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://larsfaye.com/opengraph-expertise.jpg' data-full='https://larsfaye.com/opengraph-expertise.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49374269' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49374269' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://safedep.io/arrayref-proc-macro1-rust-build-time-malware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malicious Rust crate Arrayref runs a build-time payload</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>恶意 Rust crate Arrayref 在构建时载荷执行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49374269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49374269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49374269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A build-time payload in the Arrayref crate demonstrates how cargo&#x27;s build scripts can run arbitrary code during compilation, creating supply-chain risks for Rust projects. The incident reinforces the need for careful dependency auditing, lockfiles, and tooling that detects unusual build behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arrayref 的构建时载荷暴露了 cargo 构建脚本在编译阶段执行任意代码的风险，暴露了 Rust 项目的供应链隐患。事件强调对依赖项进行严格审计、锁定版本以及使用能检测异常构建行为的工具的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://safedep.io/images/arrayref-blog-blanner.png' data-full='https://safedep.io/images/arrayref-blog-blanner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49120097' data-tags='AI,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49120097' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/security/chrome-stronger-with-every-update/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google fixed more Chrome bugs in June than over the past two years, thanks to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌靠 AI，在六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年总和</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 564</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120097' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined. This shows how AI tooling can accelerate vulnerability response and elevate browser hardening. It could foreshadow broader shifts in security workflows across software teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌称借助 AI 的分流与补丁生成，六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年的总和。这显示出 AI 工具能加速漏洞响应、提升浏览器安全性。此举或预示软件团队在安全工作流程上的更广泛变革。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49457545' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mturk.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanical Turk shutting down September 30</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Amazon Mechanical Turk 将于9月30日停止运营</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49457545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49457545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49457545' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Mechanical Turk will shut down on Sept 30, ending a long-running crowdwork and data-labeling platform for ML. The move affects researchers and companies relying on microtasks, pushing them to alternatives or in-house labeling workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Mechanical Turk 将于9月30日停止运营。这一决策结束了长期用于众包任务和机器学习数据标注的平台，相关方需转向其他平台或自行开展标注工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mturk.com/assets/images/logo.svg' data-full='https://www.mturk.com/assets/images/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49176038' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://earendil.com/posts/pi-autoresearch-and-databricks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pi&#x27;s Minimalism Is Its Advantage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pi 的极简主义成为其优势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 288</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176038' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pi&#x27;s minimalism offers a practical performance edge in AI autoresearch workflows with Databricks. By trading feature breadth for simplicity, it can deliver predictable, approachable outcomes for developers. This design choice highlights how lean engineering can pay off in data tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pi 的极简设计带来在与 Databricks 的自动化研究工作流中可观的性能优势。以简化而非堆砌功能，能为开发者带来更可控、易上手的结果。此设计选择凸显了在数据工具中，精简也能带来实实在在的收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/pi-autoresearch-and-databricks.png' data-full='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/pi-autoresearch-and-databricks.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pixelcluster.dev/VRAM-Overcommit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 7.3 improves performance when running out of vRAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 7.3 在虚拟内存不足时提升性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 297</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49342719' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 introduces improvements for overcommit and memory management to squeeze extra performance when VRAM is exhausted, benefiting GPU-heavy workloads and servers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 在内存超额承诺与管理方面有所改进，在显存耗尽时提升性能，利于 GPU 密集型工作负载。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pixelcluster.dev/assets/images/RDNA3Microbench.png' data-full='https://pixelcluster.dev/assets/images/RDNA3Microbench.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49448819' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49448819' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/us/meta-settles-with-us-states-over-social-media-harms-2026-08-26/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta reaches $17B settlement over social media harms to children</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta就儿童在社交媒体上的伤害达成170亿美元和解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49448819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 532</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49448819' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta agrees to a $17 billion settlement with U.S. states over alleged harms to children from its social platforms. The deal includes funding for safety improvements, independent oversight, and a framework to curb risky design patterns affecting young users, underscoring regulatory pressure on big tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 与多州就其社交平台对儿童造成的有害影响达成170亿美元和解。和解包括加强安全措施、独立监督，以及限制有害设计模式的合规要求，体现了监管对大型科技公司的持续压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49213754' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49213754' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://app.dealroom.co/news/feed/oracle-bans-ai-generated-code-from-openjdk-despite-ellison-s-claim-oracle-isn-t-writing-its-own-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle 禁止 OpenJDK 使用 AI 生成的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 374</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49213754' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK. The policy signals a firm stance on machine-produced software and licensing risk, potentially complicating AI-assisted development in the Java ecosystem. It could force contributors to audit code provenance more carefully.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>甲骨文宣布禁止把 AI 生成的代码用于 OpenJDK。此举表明其在机器生成软件方面的严格治理，可能影响 Java 生态中对 AI 辅助编码的使用和许可安排，并促使开发者更加审查代码来源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://webshotter.dealroom.co/?width=1600&amp;height=800&amp;wait_until=networkidle0&amp;url=https%3A%2F%2Fapp.dealroom.co%2Fnews%2Ffeed%2Foracle-bans-ai-generated-code-from-openjdk-despite-ellison-s-claim-oracle-isn-t-writing-its-own-code%3Fscreenshot%3Dtrue' data-full='https://webshotter.dealroom.co/?width=1600&amp;height=800&amp;wait_until=networkidle0&amp;url=https%3A%2F%2Fapp.dealroom.co%2Fnews%2Ffeed%2Foracle-bans-ai-generated-code-from-openjdk-despite-ellison-s-claim-oracle-isn-t-writing-its-own-code%3Fscreenshot%3Dtrue' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49499854' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49499854' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://0xcc.io/posts/omarchy-root-creds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Omarchy: Any User Process Can Escalate to Root</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Omarchy：任意用户进程可升级为 root</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 536</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49499854' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation. The issue underscores how fragile privilege boundaries can be and emphasizes the need for rapid patches, safer defaults, and stronger containment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。问题凸显了权限边界的脆弱性，强调需要迅速修补、采用更安全的默认设置，并加强对进程的容器化或沙箱保护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49415271' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49415271' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fzakaria.com/2026/08/23/your-executable-is-a-sqlite-database' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Executable Is a SQLite Database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可执行文件就是一个 SQLite 数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49415271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49415271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49415271' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Treating an executable as a SQLite database reframes how we analyze and store binary code, enabling structured querying and tooling. The perspective suggests new ways to inspect, modify, or reason about binaries without abandoning traditional formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把可执行程序当作 SQLite 数据库来看待，能以结构化的方式对二进制代码进行分析、查询和工具化操作。这一视角可能带来新的二进制分析方法，促进对现有格式的补充性思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fzakaria.com/assets/images/get-better-material-sqlite.png' data-full='https://fzakaria.com/assets/images/get-better-material-sqlite.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49433450' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-49433450' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-unveils-a-more-powerful-mac-mini-featuring-the-all-new-m6-and-m5-pro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Mac mini, featuring M6 and M5 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果发布新款Mac mini，搭载M6与M5 Pro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49433450' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Mac mini receives a performance boost with the M6 and M5 Pro, extending strong compute capabilities in an ultra-compact form factor. The update targets developers, media workflows, and light server tasks that benefit from efficient, high‑density compute. It broadens Apple&#x27;s entry-level pro workstation options without increasing the footprint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mac mini 通过 M6 与 M5 Pro 获得性能提升，在极小的机身中扩展了计算能力。更新面向开发者、媒体工作流以及需要高效率密度计算的轻量服务器场景，提供更强的入门级 pro 工作站选择，同时保持小巧体积。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-unveils-a-more-powerful-mac-mini-featuring-the-all-new-m6-and-m5-pro/tile/Apple-Mac-mini-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-unveils-a-more-powerful-mac-mini-featuring-the-all-new-m6-and-m5-pro/tile/Apple-Mac-mini-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zed.dev/deltadb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zed DeltaDB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zed DeltaDB 数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49187256' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed DeltaDB pitches a delta-first database approach that emphasizes incremental updates and efficient data synchronization. This could appeal to applications with heavy write workloads or distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 推出 DeltaDB，强调以增量更新为核心的数据库方案，提升数据同步效率。该思路对高写入负载或分布式系统的场景尤具吸引力。</p>
